# Project Source Link Inventory

This inventory extracts URLs found in the five uploaded project workbooks. Exact duplicate URL strings are listed once, with all distinct workbook locations retained.

**Unique URL strings extracted: 219**

## Scope and notes

- Links are grouped by the workbook sheet/topic in which they appeared.
- Descriptive labels are copied from nearby spreadsheet cells; they are context, not independent verification of the linked content.
- Some links are secondary sources, archived copies, or may need checking before being cited as scientific evidence.
- URL strings are deduplicated; different URLs that lead to the same resource are retained separately.

## Workbooks checked

- `Lunar_ML_Data_Template.xlsx` — 4942 distinct URL-bearing cells
- `Mars_ ML_Data_Template.xlsx` — 0 distinct URL-bearing cells
- `mars_classification_reference.xlsx` — 0 distinct URL-bearing cells
- `Mars_Geographical_Features_Database.xlsx` — 17 distinct URL-bearing cells
- `mars_moon_planetary_database.xlsx` — 415 distinct URL-bearing cells
- `Mars_ ML_Data_Template.xlsx` — no embedded URLs found.
- `mars_classification_reference.xlsx` — no embedded URLs found.

Earlier project discussions mention NASA Earthdata, Mars Trek, NASA/JPL planetary datasets, LRO/LROC, Diviner, MOLA, THEMIS, CRISM, HiRISE, TES, HRSC, LOLA, Mini-RF, Kaguya/SELENE, and the USGS Gazetteer of Planetary Nomenclature.
